*Cheng-Wei Chiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了type-II seesaw模型中Higgs三重态双电荷与单电荷成员之间的质量劈裂。电弱圈修正将其固定在767-841 MeV范围（在λ₄=0且重整化标度μ̄=M_{H±±}时），标度移动两倍会使圈值在200 GeV时变化208 MeV、在1 TeV时变化42 MeV。在852 MeV的参考劈裂下，H±±→H±W±(*)衰变占总宽度的40%（200 GeV）和7%（500 GeV），此时部分子计数方法失效，对率的高估因子为1.46±0.05（852 MeV）或低估因子4.87±0.09（200 MeV）。混合同号拓扑的存在与否取决于λ₄，该通道使两个已发表的排除边界分别移动-1.4%和+24.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在II型跷跷板模型的唯象学研究中，Higgs三重态中双电荷与单电荷成员的质量差通常被当作自由参数处理。本文旨在通过电弱圈修正确定该质量劈裂的理论预测值，并评估不同劈裂值对物理观测量的影响，特别是对H±±→H±W±(*)衰变率和实验排除边界的修正。

Method: 通过在重整化标度μ̄=M_{H±±}处设定四叉耦合λ₄=0，计算电弱圈修正以确定质量劈裂。研究了重整化标度在±2倍范围内变化对圈值的影响，并对H±±→H±W±(*)衰变的部分子计数方法与基于强子数据的精确计算进行对比，分析不同衰变通道对实验排除边界的影响。

Result: 质量劈裂被电弱圈固定为767-841 MeV；标度变化导致圈值有208 MeV（200 GeV）到42 MeV（1 TeV）的移动。852 MeV参考劈裂下，该衰变占总宽度40%（200 GeV）或7%（500 GeV）。部分子计数在852 MeV时高估率1.46±0.05倍，在200 MeV时低估4.87±0.09倍。该通道使两个已发表排除边界分别改变-1.4%和+24.5%。

Conclusion: type-II seesaw模型中Higgs三重态的质量劈裂并非真正的自由参数，而是由电弱圈效应决定的，同时λ₄对混合同号拓扑信号的存在起决定作用。质量劈裂值对衰变率和实验排除边界的影响显著，需要精确的计算方法而非部分子计数近似。

Abstract: The mass splitting between the doubly and singly charged members of the Higgs triplet of the type-II seesaw model is usually treated as a free parameter in the collider literature. Electroweak loops fix it to $767$ to $841$~MeV over the mass range considered here once the quartic coupling $λ_4$ is set to zero at the renormalization scale $\overlineμ= M_{H^{\pm\pm}}$. Moving that scale by a factor of two in either direction moves the loop value by $208$~MeV at $200$~GeV, falling to $42$~MeV at $1$~TeV. At the reference splitting of $852$~MeV, the transition $H^{\pm\pm} \to H^\pm W^{\pm(*)}$ takes $40\%$ ($7\%$) of the total width at the crossover triplet vacuum expectation value for a doubly charged Higgs mass of $200$~GeV ($500$~GeV). That splitting is also where the partonic counting of that rate fails. Evaluated from hadronic data, the counting overestimates the rate by a factor $1.46 \pm 0.05$ at a splitting of $852$~MeV and underestimates it by a factor $4.87 \pm 0.09$ at $200$~MeV. Whether the mixed same-sign topology survives is then a condition on $λ_4$. The same channel moves the two published exclusion boundaries in opposite directions, by $-1.4\%$ and $+24.5\%$, at the level of branching ratios and at fixed experimental acceptance.

</details>


### [99] [Dielectric Response for Light Dark Matter Direct Detection Beyond the Longitudinal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20715)
*Zheng-Liang Liang,Lei Wu,Wen-Na Yang,Lin Zhang,Bin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机相位近似下完成了半导体中光暗物质-电子散射横向介电函数ε_T的完整推导与定量评估。对于硅材料，体等离子激元区域横向能量损失函数比纵向低4-6个数量级，首次严格证明了传统纵向近似的合理性；但在沉积能量ω≲1 eV（低于硅探测器可靠产生一个电子-空穴对的能量阈值）的能区出现可观的横向修正，为现有纵向计算提供了定量误差评估，并识别了相对论暗物质解释中必须纳入横向修正的运动学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光暗物质-电子散射的介电常数形式化仅使用纵向介电函数ε_L，横向响应ε_T一直被定性理由忽略，但缺乏完整的推导与定量评估，导致该近似缺乏严格证明且未知其适用范围。

Method: 在均匀电子气体的随机相位近似（RPA）下，对横向介电函数ε_T进行完整的解析推导，并通过硅材料的数值计算，将横向与纵向能量损失函数对比，评估横向修正的大小及其在不同能量区间的行为。

Result: 硅材料在体等离子激元能区，横向能量损失函数比纵向低4-6个数量级，首次为传统纵向近似提供了严格依据；然而在ω≲1 eV的低能区出现显著的横向修正，该能区低于硅探测器可靠产生单电子-空穴对的阈值，故对常规探测器无实质影响。

Conclusion: 纵向近似在等离子激元主导能区是严格合理的，但相对论暗物质解释中需注意低能横向修正的贡献；本文提供了对现有纵向计算的定量误差评估及需纳入横向修正的运动学区域指引。

Abstract: The dielectric formalism for light dark matter--electron scattering in semiconductors has, to date, employed only the longitudinal dielectric function $ε_L$, with the transverse response $ε_T$ universally neglected on qualitative grounds. A complete derivation and quantitative evaluation of $ε_T$ in this context has been lacking. We provide this derivation within the random phase approximation for a homogeneous electron gas. For silicon, we find that the transverse energy loss function is $4--6$ orders of magnitude below the longitudinal one in the bulk plasmon regime, providing the first rigorous justification for the conventional longitudinal approximation. The sizable transverse corrections appear for deposited energies $ω\lesssim1\,\mathrm{eV}$, which lies below the energy required to reliably produce one electron-hole pair in silicon detectors. Our results provide a quantitative error assessment for existing longitudinal calculations and identify kinematic regimes where transverse corrections ought to be included for relativistic dark-matter interpretations.

</details>


### [100] [Qubits for Dark Matter Hunting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20745)
*Takeo Moroi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于使用量子比特及其他量子激发探测暗物质（及其他超越标准模型物理）的入门综述，系统介绍了量子比特的基本性质、暗物质电场激发机制、Jaynes-Cummings耦合量子比特-腔系统动力学、马尔可夫噪声与退相干，以及量子传感的核心概念（如Cramér-Rao界、标准量子极限、海森堡极限）和纠缠态对暗物质探测灵敏度的增强潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质探测是粒子物理与天体物理的核心前沿问题，而量子信息技术的快速发展为暗物质探测提供了全新的途径。本文旨在为有兴趣将量子比特等量子激发应用于暗物质（及其他超出标准模型的物理）探测的研究者提供系统性的入门介绍，架起量子信息与粒子物理之间的桥梁。

Method: 本文采用综述性的方法，逐一介绍量子探测暗物质所涉及的关键理论框架：量子比特的基础性质；暗物质诱导电场激发量子比特的机制（强调暗物质相干性的影响）；Jaynes-Cummings模型描述的量子比特-腔耦合系统动力学；Lindblad方程描述的马尔可夫噪声与退相干影响；并引入量子传感的核心概念，包括算子求和表示、正算子值测量（POVM）、Cramér-Rao界、标准量子极限、海森堡极限以及纠缠态对灵敏度的潜在增强。

Result: 文章系统梳理了量子比特探测暗物质的理论路径，特别聚焦于波状暗物质（wave-like dark matter）的探测场景，阐明了量子传感理论框架如何为暗物质探测灵敏度提供定量分析与优化指导。

Conclusion: 该综述为量子信息与暗物质探测的交叉领域提供了入门级但全面的理论框架，强调量子比特作为暗物质探测器的可行性，并指出利用纠缠态等量子资源有望突破标准量子极限，实现对波状暗物质更灵敏的探测。

Abstract: An introductory review is provided for those who are interested in exploring applications of qubits and other quantum excitations to the detection of dark matter (and any other physics beyond the Standard Model). Topics covered include the fundamental properties of qubits, the excitation mechanism of qubits due to the electric field induced by dark matter (with attention to the effects of the coherence of dark matter), the dynamics of coupled qubit-cavity systems modeled by the Jaynes-Cummings framework, and the influence of noise and decoherence (especially Markovian noise described by the Lindblad equation). In addition, the article introduces essential concepts in quantum sensing, including the operator-sum representation and positive operator-valued measures, the Cramér-Rao bound, the standard quantum limit and the Heisenberg limit, and the potential enhancement of sensitivity to dark matter achievable with entangled states. Throughout, these topics are discussed with particular emphasis on their application to the detection of wave-like dark matter.

</details>


### [101] [Anomaly-free axion-like particle in Nelson-Barr models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20773)
*Mohammad Aghaie,Ryoma Masuda,Ryosuke Sato*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有离散Z_N对称性的Nelson-Barr模型，该模型通过自发CP破缺解决强CP问题，并自然预言一个无反常的轻轴子样粒子（ALP）。该ALP虽然与QCD轴子不同不耦合于光子和胶子，但通过CKM矩阵决定的味破坏耦合与夸克相互作用，诱导稀有介子衰变。研究发现冻结入机制所解释的暗物质丰度区域受精密味实验和恒星冷却约束严格限制，而错位机制在广泛参数空间内仍是稳健可行的暗物质产生机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nelson-Barr模型通过自发CP破缺解决强CP问题，而该机制是否自然预言新的可观测粒子及其暗物质候选体尚未被充分探索。作者旨在揭示该模型中自然存在一个无反常ALP，并系统研究其宇宙学产生机制、实验可探测性和对Nelson-Barr机制本身的探针意义。

Method: 作者在具有Z_N离散对称性的Nelson-Barr框架中推导出轻ALP的存在及其耦合结构。通过分析ALP与夸克的味破坏耦合（由CKM矩阵决定），计算其诱导的稀有介子衰变。分别采用冻结入（freeze-in）和错位（misalignment）两种机制研究ALP在宇宙学中的产生，并结合精密味实验、SN1987A恒星冷却、结构形成观测和X射线观测等约束来划定可行参数空间。

Result: 冻结入机制下能解释观测暗物质丰度的参数空间受到精密味实验和SN1987A恒星冷却约束的强烈限制，仅存很小的可行区域，该区域将被Vera Rubin天文台和Athena、GECCO、THESEUS等下一代X射线任务全面检验。相比之下，错位机制在广泛的参数空间内仍能稳健地产生观测到的暗物质丰度。该ALP对光子和胶子耦合高度压低，因此自然长寿命。

Conclusion: 精密味测量、宇宙学观测和X射线搜索为这个无反常ALP提供了互补的探测途径，从而也为Nelson-Barr解决强CP问题的方案提供了间接检验手段。错位机制是该ALP作为暗物质的主导产生机制，而冻结入机制则面临严格但可检验的约束。

Abstract: We study Nelson--Barr models with a discrete $Z_N$ symmetry that solve the strong CP problem through spontaneous CP violation, and show that they naturally predict a light axion-like particle (ALP) without introducing any additional ingredients. Unlike the QCD axion, this ALP is anomaly-free: its couplings to photons and gluons are highly suppressed, rendering it naturally long-lived. Instead, it couples to quarks through flavor-violating interactions whose structure is dictated by the CKM matrix. These interactions induce rare meson decays, providing a unique probe of the Nelson--Barr mechanism. We study the cosmological production of the ALP through both freeze-in and misalignment mechanisms. We show that the parameter space in which the observed relic abundance is explained by the freeze-in mechanism is subject to stringent constraints from precision flavor experiments and stellar cooling bounds from SN1987A, leaving only a small viable region that will be comprehensively tested by future structure-formation observations such as the Vera Rubin Observatory and next-generation X-ray missions like Athena, GECCO and THESEUS. In contrast, misalignment production remains a robust and viable mechanism for explaining the observed dark matter abundance over a broad region of parameter space. Our results demonstrate that precision flavor measurements, cosmological observations, and X-ray searches provide complementary probes of this anomaly-free ALP and, consequently, of the Nelson--Barr solution to the strong CP problem.

</details>


### [102] [The role of triangle singularity in the $B^0 \to D^- π^+ a_0(980)(π^0 η)$ decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20835)
*Wei Wang,Dazhuang He,Xuan Luo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究B^0 → D^- π^+ a_0(980)(π^0 η)衰变中的三角机制，验证a_1(1420)的三角奇异性解释。通过K̄*0-K^+-K^-三角圈，三角振幅在1420 MeV附近产生显著峰，π^0 η不变质量谱在980 MeV附近呈窄峰，π^+π^0 η不变质量分布在1420 MeV处出现清晰峰，进一步证实a_1(1420)的三角奇异性起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMPASS合作组观测到的a_1(1420)的三角奇异性解释已被广泛接受，但需要通过其他衰变过程来进一步验证该解释。作者希望利用B介子衰变这一全新平台来检验三角奇异性机制，因为B^0 → D^- K^+ K̄*0衰变已被Belle实验观测到，为三角机制研究提供了实验基础。

Method: 作者将a_0(980)视为动力学产生的态，考虑B^0 → D^- K^+ K̄*0衰变后的K̄*0 → K^- π^+级联衰变构成的K̄*0-K^+-K^-三角圈机制。计算了三角振幅、微分散射宽度以及各中间态的不变质量分布，分析三角奇异性在π a_0(980)、π^0 η和π^+ π^0 η系统中的表现。

Result: 三角振幅在1420 MeV附近出现显著峰，体现在π a_0(980)系统的不变质量谱中；π^0 η不变质量分布呈现在980 MeV附近的窄峰；π^+ π^0 η系统的不变质量分布在1420 MeV处显示清晰峰，证实了三角奇异性对a_1(1420)的解释。

Conclusion: B^0介子衰变模式可作为进一步探索a_1(1420)三角奇异性本性的潜在平台，该预言可在LHCb、BESIII和Belle II等未来实验中得到检验。

Abstract: The triangle singularity interpretation of the \replyhe{$a_1(1420)$} observed by the COMPASS Collaboration has been widely accepted. In this work, we investigate the triangle mechanism in the decay $B^0 \to D^- π^+ a_0(980)(π^0 η)$, where the $a_0(980)$ is treated as a dynamically generated state. The $\bar{K}^{*0}$-$K^+$-$K^-$ triangle loop originates from the decay $B^0 \to D^- K^+ \bar{K}^{*0}$, which has been observed by the Belle Collaboration, followed by the subsequent decay $\bar{K}^{*0} \to K^- π^+$. The triangle amplitude develops a pronounced peak around 1420 MeV, which is reflected in the invariant mass spectrum of the $πa_0(980)$ system. The differential decay width is calculated and exhibits a narrow peak around $980~\mathrm{MeV}$ in the $π^0 η$ invariant mass distribution. Furthermore, the invariant mass distribution of the $π^+ π^0 η$ system shows a clear peak around $1420~\mathrm{MeV}$, which further \replyhe{confirms} the triangle singularity explanation of the \replyhe{$a_1(1420)$}. We expect that the proposed $B^0$ decay mode could provide a potential platform for further exploring the triangle-singularity nature of the \replyhe{$a_1(1420)$} and could be tested in future experiments such as LHCb, BESIII, and Belle~II.

</details>


### [103] [The excited baryon spectrum from a unified quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20892)
*Yan-Ke Chen,Liang-Zhen Wen,Wei-Lin Wu,Lu Meng,Shi-Lin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含自旋-轨道和张量相互作用的非相对论组分夸克势模型，用最新实验数据重新拟合参数（排除激发态轻强子和奇特强子候选态），成功复现了所有拟合扇区的介子和重子谱（绝大多数偏差低于20 MeV）。用同一参数预测了轨道和径向量子数激发的重子谱与内部结构，但激发态轻强子谱与实验偏差显著，模型未能解决这一长期争议，从而界定了该模型的适用范围，为多夸克态少体计算提供受控起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多夸克态需要可靠的夸克势模型，该模型必须用单一参数集同时描述所有味道扇区的介子与重子谱（含轨道和径向激发），但现有模型均不满足此要求，因此需要构建并重新拟合满足该条件的模型。

Method: 构建包含自旋-轨道与张量相互作用的非相对论组分夸克势模型；排除激发态轻强子和奇特强子候选态后，用最新实验数据重新拟合模型参数；用同一参数集统一计算各味道扇区的介子、重子光谱，并预测激发态重子和轻重子谱。

Result: 拟合参数成功复现所有拟合扇区谱，大多数偏差低于20 MeV，最大偏差在几十MeV量级（非相对论模型的典型精度）；预测了激发重子谱与内部结构；但激发态轻重子谱与实验偏差显著，长期争议未能解决。

Conclusion: 该重构模型界定了非相对论组分夸克势模型的适用范围，为多夸克态少体计算提供了统一受控的起点；同时呼吁实验上对预测态进行搜寻。

Abstract: A common approach to studying the multiquark states is to solve the few-body Schrödinger equation within a quark potential model. The multiquark states may contain quarks with several different flavors and have richer color structures than ordinary hadrons. The reliability of such an investigation requires that the underlying quark potential model can simultaneously describe the meson and baryon spectra across all flavor sectors, including orbital and radial excitations, with a single parameter set. At present, no quark potential model fully satisfies this requirement. We construct a simple nonrelativistic constituent quark potential model that includes spin--orbit and tensor interactions. We refit the model parameters to the latest experimental data. The excited light hadrons and several exotic hadron candidates are excluded from the fit. The resulting parameter set reproduces the spectra across all fitted sectors. The vast majority of deviations are below 20 MeV, while the largest deviations remain of the order of several tens of MeV, which is the typical accuracy of nonrelativistic quark potential models. Using the same parameters, we predict the spectra and internal structures of the orbitally and radially excited heavy baryons, which await further experimental determination. The excited light baryons are calculated with the same parameters. The calculated excited light baryon spectra deviate substantially from experimental values. Our refitted model does not resolve these longstanding discrepancies. The results delimit the range of validity of the nonrelativistic constituent quark potential model. By treating mesons and baryons across all flavor sectors, including both orbital and radial excitations within a unified framework, the model provides a controlled starting point for few-body calculations of multiquark states. We also urge experimental searches for the predicted states.

</details>


### [104] [The ABC of RPV III: Classification of R-parity Violating Signatures from LQD Couplings and their Coverage at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21033)
*Herbi K. Dreiner,Yong Sheng Koay,Tina Potter,Martin Schürmann,Rhitaja Sengupta,Apoorva Shah,Nadja Strobbe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了R宇称破坏的最小超对称标准模型（RPV-MSSM）中LQ\bar{D}算符的唯象学和实验现状，作为ABC of RPV系列的第三项研究。作者对所有可能的LSP和八种代表性耦合进行分类，评估了当前LHC通过ATLAS和CMS搜索（用CheckMATE 2实现）对该模型的覆盖情况，发现对色子扇区有显著灵敏度，但wino/higgsino型LSP（涉及τ轻子耦合）和slepton LSP仍存在覆盖空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超标准模型（BSM）搜索在LHC上的覆盖范围需要系统评估。此前已有对LL\bar{E}和\bar{U}\bar{D}\bar{D}算符的研究，本文针对LQ\bar{D}算符进行系统研究，以评估当前LHC搜索能力对该具体RPV通道的覆盖程度，并识别实验搜索盲区。

Method: 以RPV-MSSM为测试模型，对所有可能的LSP类型和八个代表性的LQ\bar{D}耦合进行分类，同时考虑直接LSP产生和通过规范级联的产生路径。假设每次只有一个非零的LQ\bar{D}耦合，使用ATLAS和CMS搜索在CheckMATE 2中实现重新解释，评估当前LHC数据覆盖情况，并识别可用于未来重铸的最新搜索。

Result: 发现对色子扇区（colored sector）具有显著的灵敏度；对涉及τ轻子耦合的wino和higgsino型LSP存在覆盖空白，尤其当LSP是唯一在LHC运动学极限内的超粒子时；slepton LSP在其直接产生下仍未被约束，但对具有bino型LSP的slepton NLSP有一定灵敏度。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了CheckMATE 2搜索数据库的空白，以及针对特定LQ\bar{D}特征的LHC定向搜索的必要性。这些发现为未来实验搜索策略和理论模型约束提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We consider the R-parity violating Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (RPV-MSSM) with its wide range of signatures as a test model for the coverage of beyond the Standard Model searches at the LHC. Here we present a systematic study of the phenomenological and experimental status of specifically the R-parity violating $LQ\bar D$ operators, as the third study in the ABC of RPV series, following our previous analyses of the $LL\bar E$ and $\bar U\bar D\bar D$ operators. We classify the distinct collider signatures for all possible LSPs and for eight representative $LQ\bar D$ couplings, considering both direct LSP production and production through gauge cascades. Assuming one non-zero $LQ\bar D$ coupling at a time, we assess the current LHC coverage using ATLAS and CMS searches implemented in $\texttt{CheckMATE 2}$, and identify recent searches relevant for future recasting. We find substantial sensitivity to the colored sector, while gaps remain for wino- and higgsino-like LSPs for couplings involving $τ$ leptons, when the LSP is the only sparticle within the LHC kinematic limit. Slepton LSPs remain unconstrained in their direct production at the LHC, although some sensitivity is found for slepton NLSPs with a bino-like LSP. These results highlight gaps in the $\texttt{CheckMATE 2}$ search database, as well as the need for targeted LHC searches for specific $LQ\bar D$ signatures.

</details>


### [105] [Unraveling QCD dynamics with heavy quark energy correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21145)
*Ivan Vitev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了重味喷注的能量-能量关联子（EECs），揭示了有限夸克质量如何重塑喷注内部辐射模式（如小角度抑制和死锥效应），并通过有效场论将该计算扩展到核反应中，展示了介质诱导辐射与真空质量抑制之间的竞争导致喷注子结构发生非平庸修正，可用于当前设施的小碰撞系统中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味喷注及其子结构是研究夸克质量在QCD辐射及核物质中修饰作用的独特窗口。此前喷注能量-能量关联子的计算主要针对无质量夸克，缺乏对有限质量的完整处理，尤其缺乏在核介质中质量效应与介质效应相互作用的系统研究。

Method: 利用有效场论（EFT）分析方法，对重夸克能量-能量关联子进行显式含质量效应的计算；将该计算扩展到与原子核的碰撞反应中，分析介质诱导辐射与真空质量抑制的竞争机制；针对粲味和底味喷注分别分析EECs修正行为。

Result: 证明了粲夸克和底夸克的有限质量会重塑喷注内部辐射角分布，在小角度产生特征性抑制，并导致与无质量喷注预期可测量的偏差。在核反应中，识别出EECs由重夸克质量主导的参数区间，此时该可观测量在QCD物质中表现出定性和定量上不同的行为。

Conclusion: 重味喷注EECs可作为探测质量效应与介质效应耦合的灵敏探针；该形式论可在当前实验设施的小碰撞系统中进行检验，为重子物质中重夸克能量损失机制提供新的约束。

Abstract: Heavy-flavor jets and their substructure provide a unique window into the role of quark mass on QCD radiation and its modification in nuclear matter. In this work, we investigate heavy-quark energy-energy correlators (EECs) that are explicitly sensitive to mass effects, focusing on angular distributions, energy flow, and the dead-cone effect. We demonstrate how the finite mass of charm and bottom quarks reshapes the intra-jet radiation pattern, leading to characteristic suppression at small angles and measurable deviations from massless jet expectations. Using effective field theory analysis, we extend the calculation of these observables to reactions with nuclei to show that medium-induced radiation competes with vacuum mass suppression, resulting in a nontrivial modification of jet substructure. Specifically, we identify regimes where EECs are dominated by the heavy quark mass, leading to qualitatively and quantitatively different behavior of this observable in QCD matter. We discuss the charm and bottom jet energy-energy correlators modification in reactions with nuclei, and further demonstrate how the formalism can be tested in small collision system at current facilities.

</details>


### [106] [The electron parton distribution functions at the NNLO in QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21181)
*Stefano Frixione,Kay Schönwald*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QED框架下，分别于标准MS（修正最小减除）方案和Δ方案中计算了电子部分子分布函数（PDFs）到次次领头阶（NNLO），将Δ方案推广到全阶定义，并证明该方案中软对数增强完全消失，有助于简化数值模拟和分析计算。此外，作者论证了对于质心能量在百GeV量级的对撞机，NNLO PDFs对于达到10⁻⁴或更高的相对精度目标是必需的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子PDFs是轻子对撞机中collinear-factorisation框架下截面计算的核心组成部分。现有的计算主要停留在NLO水平，且标准MS方案中的软对数增强使得数值模拟和分析计算复杂度较高。本文旨在推广Δ方案到全阶，以简化计算并提升精度。

Method: 作者在QED框架下完成了电子PDFs在NNLO阶的计算，对比了两种因子化方案：标准MS方案与Δ方案。Δ方案原定义于NLO阶，本工作将其推广到全阶（所有阶）。作者给出了这两种方案中PDFs在大-z（z→1）行为下的解析结果，并分析了软对数增强的差异。

Result: 解析结果表明，在Δ方案中，MS方案所特有的软对数增强被完全消除，从而显著降低数值模拟和分析计算的复杂度。作者进一步论证，对质心能量在百GeV量级的对撞机，若要达到10⁻⁴或以上的相对精度，NNLO电子PDFs不可或缺。

Conclusion: Δ方案因其消除软对数增强的特性，是电子PDF计算及软重求和物理观测量中更自然的选择，能大幅简化计算复杂性。同时，NNLO阶的电子PDFs是实现百GeV级对撞机高精度（10⁻⁴）物理预测的必要条件。

Abstract: The electron Parton Distribution Functions (PDFs) are essential components in the calculations of cross sections within a collinear-factorisation framework at lepton colliders. We compute them at the next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in QED by working in two different factorisation schemes, namely the standard $\overline{\rm MS}$ one and the so-called $Δ$ scheme, which was originally defined at the next-to-leading order, and which we generalise in this work by extending its definition to all orders. We present analytical results relevant to the large-$z$ behaviour of the PDFs in both factorisation schemes, and thus show how the soft-logarithm enhancement of the $\overline{\rm MS}$ electron PDF is completely absent in the $Δ$ scheme. The latter therefore constitutes a natural choice that helps significantly reduce the complexity not only of numerical simulations in phenomenological applications, but also that of analytical computations necessary to achieve the soft resummation of physical observables. We argue that NNLO PDFs are necessary to attain relative-precision targets of $10^{-4}$ or better, for colliders whose centre-of-mass energies are in the hundred-GeV range.

</details>


### [107] [Higgs Boson plus Quark Pair Production at High Energies as di-Higgs Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21195)
*Andreas Maier,Giacomo Ruisi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了高能QCD重求和（resummation）对大型强子对撞机（LHC）上希格斯玻色子产生过程的影响，首次在胶子融合（gluon-fusion）通道中研究伴随底夸克对和两个额外硬喷注的希格斯玻色子产生，将其作为矢量玻色子融合（VBF）希格斯对产生的背景。研究发现重求和效应在VBF-like相空间区域能显著修正QCD贡献，表明固定阶计算高估了胶子融合背景的污染。此外，论文首次将夸克对重求和效应纳入High Energy Jets（HEJ）框架下希格斯玻色子伴随喷注产生的预测中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在精确评估高能QCD重求和效应对LHC上希格斯对产生相关过程的影响，特别是澄清VBF希格斯对产生实验中来自胶子融合通道背景的污染程度，从而为实验测量提供更可靠的理论预测。

Method: 论文采用高能QCD重求和（high-energy resummation）方法，利用HEJ（High Energy Jets）框架进行研究。研究涵盖两个部分：一是首次计算胶子融合通道中希格斯玻色子伴随底夸克对和两个额外硬喷注的产生过程，作为VBF希格斯对产生的背景进行量化评估；二是将quark-pair重求和效应首次纳入HEJ对希格斯伴随喷注产生的预测中，扩展了该框架的适用范围。

Result: 研究发现高能重求和效应在VBF-like相空间区域能够显著改变QCD贡献的大小，固定阶次计算会高估来自胶子融合背景的污染。同时，扩展后的HEJ框架（首次包含quark-pair重求和效应）提供了更精确的希格斯伴随喷注产生预测。

Conclusion: 高能QCD重求和对VBF希格斯对产生实验中的背景评估至关重要，采用重求和修正后的预测能更真实地反映胶子融合背景的贡献，有助于提高未来LHC上希格斯对产生测量的精度与信噪比。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of high-energy QCD resummation on Higgs-boson production processes relevant for Higgs-pair studies at the Large Hadron Collider. We present the first dedicated study of Higgs boson production in association with a bottom-quark pair and two additional hard jets in the gluon-fusion channel at high energies as a background to vector-boson-fusion Higgs-pair production. The resummation can significantly modify the QCD contribution in VBF-like regions of phase space, indicating that fixed-order calculations overestimate the contamination from gluon-fusion backgrounds. We then extend existing High Energy Jets (HEJ) predictions for Higgs boson production in association with jets by including quark-pair resummation effects for the first time.

</details>


### [108] [Implications of two-channel rescattering for charm CP violation from precise dispersive data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21212)
*Antonio Pich,Eleftheria Solomonidi,Luiz Vale Silva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过优化利用分支比信息和仅一个强散射输入参数，检验了数据驱动方法能否解释粲介子强子衰变中观测到的大CP破坏，发现该框架仍难以解释实验信号，并给出了限制π和K通道CP不对称性的求和规则预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观测到的粲介子强子衰变中的大CP破坏在标准模型内仍缺乏理论解释，而现有的数据驱动方法（考虑π-K末态重散射）虽能提供预测，但其依赖的输入参数存在较大不确定性。作者希望通过优化利用已有信息来检验该方法的解释能力。

Method: 充分利用已有的分支比信息，并仅采用一个确定良好的强散射输入参数，在此最小化参数依赖性下重新进行计算和预测，同时构建约束π和K通道CP不对称性的求和规则。

Result: 即使在这种最优化的参数利用方式下，数据驱动方法仍难以解释实验观测到的大CP破坏信号；同时获得了对π和K通道CP不对称性的求和规则约束预测。

Conclusion: 数据驱动重散射框架在现有输入条件下无法充分解释粲介子衰变中的大CP破坏实验信号，需要新的理论框架或机制来理解和描述这一现象，而求和规则预测可为未来实验提供可检验的约束。

Abstract: The experimental observation of large CP violation in charm-meson hadronic decays remains theoretically unexplained within the Standard Model. The data-driven approach which accounts for the rescattering between the final-state pion and kaon pairs provides predictions that fall short of the experimental values. However, it relies on a set of inputs that exhibit large uncertainties. In this work, we make optimal use of the available information on branching ratios and of only one strong-scattering input parameter that is well determined. We find that it remains unlikely to explain the experimental signal within this approach and obtain predictions for sum rules that constrain CP asymmetries of the pion and kaon channels.

</details>


### [109] [Efficient Event Generation for High-Multiplicity LHC Processes: An End-to-End GPU Workflow with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21338)
*Enrico Bothmann,Joshua Isaacson,Claudius Krause,Carla J. López-Zurita,Maximilian Spannring,Daohan Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端GPU驻留的事件生成工作流，将归一化流提议与parton级事件生成器Pepper集成，用于高多重性对撞机物理中的大规模未加权事件生成。通过在四个H100 GPU上为五个基准过程生成10^9个未加权事件，相比独立Pepper实现了高达两个数量级的端到端加速，将数周任务缩短至亚日计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高多重性过程的超大规模未加权事件样本生成受到昂贵的矩阵元评估和低未加权效率的限制，导致蒙特卡洛统计瓶颈。现有方法无法高效利用GPU资源进行端到端事件生成，亟需新的工作流来克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种GPU驻留的端到端事件生成工作流：(1) 使用螺旋度条件耦合流(h.coupling flows)作为提议分布，在线更新并辅以样本重放进行训练；(2) Python控制层与Pepper通过设备内存直接交换流生成的相空间点和目标密度评估；(3) 控制层负责流采样、提议密度评估和未加权，Pepper负责矩阵元、PDF和相空间因子的目标密度计算及事件输出；(4) 比较了子过程特定流与分组条件流（在具有相关parton内容的子过程间共享参数）两种策略。

Result: 在四个H100 GPU上成功为pp→e^+e^-+4j、pp→e^+e^-+5j、pp→tt̄+4j、pp→4j和pp→5j五个基准过程各生成10^9个未加权事件。包括流训练成本在内，实现了相比独立Pepper高达两个数量级的端到端加速，将数周任务变为亚日计算。

Conclusion: 该工作流使十亿级事件生产变得更加实用，为缓解高多重性对撞机物理中的蒙特卡洛统计瓶颈提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Producing very large unweighted event samples for high-multiplicity processes is limited by expensive matrix-element evaluations and low unweighting efficiencies. We present the first end-to-end GPU-resident event-generation workflow that integrates normalizing-flow proposals with the parton-level event generator Pepper. Helicity-conditioned coupling flows are trained using online updates supplemented by sample replay and deployed across all subprocesses of complete proton--proton collision processes with many final-state jets. In this workflow, a Python-based control layer and Pepper exchange flow-generated phase-space points and the corresponding target-density evaluations directly in device memory. The control layer performs flow sampling, proposal-density evaluation, and unweighting, while Pepper evaluates the matrix elements, PDFs, and phase-space factors defining the target density and writes the accepted events in standard formats. We compare subprocess-specific flows, with one flow per partonic subprocess, to grouped conditional flows that share parameters among subprocesses with related parton content. The workflow is benchmarked for $pp \to e^+e^- + 4j$, $pp \to e^+e^- + 5j$, $pp \to t \bar t + 4j$, $pp \to 4j$, and $pp \to 5j$ production. On four H100 GPUs, we generate $10^9$ unweighted events for each benchmark process. Including the cost of flow training, the workflow achieves end-to-end speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over standalone Pepper event generation and turns a multi-week task into a sub-day computation. It thereby makes billion-event production more practical and offers a pathway to alleviating the Monte Carlo statistics bottleneck in high-multiplicity collider physics.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [The Landau-Dirac operator with shell interactions: self-adjointness and clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20665)
*Badreddine Benhellal,Vincent Bruneau,Pablo Miranda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维Dirac算子受常磁场扰动且与光滑闭曲线上变系数静电和Lorentz标量delta相互作用耦合的谱性质，主要结果是非临界情形下离散谱在每个Landau-Dirac能级处按曲线对数容度速率聚集，并发现聚集侧随临界值相对位置变化的令人惊讶的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维Dirac算子在磁场与delta型边界扰动下的谱结构，特别是非临界与临界情况下本质谱和离散谱的行为，以及缺陷曲线几何性质对谱聚集速率的影响。

Method: 利用边界积分算子的拟微分性质，将Dirac算子自伴性分为非临界和临界情形进行分析；通过边界积分方程技术研究离散谱在Landau-Dirac能级处的聚集行为，并使用对数容度刻画聚集速率。

Result: 非临界情形下本质谱保持不变（仍为Landau-Dirac能级集），临界情形下谱隙中额外出现一段本质谱区间。主要结果显示非临界情形下离散谱在每个Landau-Dirac能级处以曲线对数容度决定的速率聚集，且聚集侧随相对临界值的位置而变化，这一现象是新颖且出乎意料的。此外，通过受限耦合获得一族外部边值问题的特征值簇，无限质量边界条件作为特例出现。

Conclusion: 本文给出了二维Dirac算子在磁场与delta型相互作用下完整的光谱分析，揭示了聚集侧随临界值位置变化的非平凡相变现象，并通过对数容度建立了曲线几何与谱聚集速率的精确联系。

Abstract: We consider the two-dimensional Dirac operator with constant magnetic field that is perturbed by a combination of electrostatic and Lorentz-scalar delta interactions with variable coefficients supported on a smooth closed curve. Self-adjointness is studied in the so called non critical and critical cases. In the non-critical case the essential spectrum is unchanged - it remains to be the set of the Landau-Dirac levels, the eigenvalues of infinite multiplicity of the unperturbed operator - while in the critical case an additional interval of essential spectrum emerges in the spectral gap containing zero. Our main result concerns the discrete spectrum in the non-critical case: using the pseudodifferential properties of the involved boundary integral operators, we show that the eigenvalues accumulate at each Landau-Dirac level at a rate governed by the logarithmic capacity of the curve. A novel and surprising phenomenon is the change in the side of the accumulation depending on the position relative to the critical value. As a byproduct, clusters of eigenvalues for a family of exterior boundary value problems are obtained via confining couplings; the infinite-mass boundary condition arises as a special case.

</details>


### [111] [On quantum channels: extreme points, topology, and categorical properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20689)
*J. M. S. T. da Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本论文从数学视角研究量子信道，主要围绕CPTP（完全正迹保持）信道与UCPTP（单态）信道的极值点构造、张量积性质、二元积与二元余积的分类，以及对极端CPTP映照集合闭包的拓扑结构（特别是CW结构）展开研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道是量子信息论的核心对象，其极值点结构对于理解信道分解与最优性至关重要。同时，从范畴论与拓扑学角度研究信道集合的结构有助于揭示量子信道空间的深层数学性质。

Method: 本文采用多种数学工具：构造各类秩的极端CPTP与UCPTP映照；利用张量积分析极端性保持问题；运用范畴论中的二元积/余积概念结合拓扑技术（如CW复形分解）进行分类与维度推导。

Result: 成功构造了任意秩的极端CPTP映照、各维数秩2及许多高阶秩的极端UCPTP映照；证明了极端CPTP映照的张量积仍为极端CPTP，但UCPTP情形不成立并给出了反例；刻画了CPTP范畴中二元积仅存在于平凡情形，并确定了二元余积存在时的维度；在极端CPTP集合闭包的拓扑研究中获得了类似于复射影空间CW结构的部分分解。

Conclusion: 本文系统推进了量子信道极值点理论、范畴结构与拓扑性质的研究，揭示了CPTP与UCPTP两类信道在数学结构上的差异，并为量子信道空间的拓扑不变量计算提供了新的工具与途径。

Abstract: In this thesis, quantum channels are studied from the point of view of Mathematics. We studied the CPTP (completely positive trace preserving) and UCPTP channels, the latter being the unital channels. In both cases, the sets are compact convex, so they are the closure of their extreme points. We constructed extreme CPTP maps for every possible rank. We also constructed extreme UCPTP maps with rank 2 for each possible dimension. For ranks of at least 3, we constructed extreme UCPTP maps for many dimensions and ranks. We also proved that the tensor product of extreme CPTP maps is an extreme CPTP map. For UCPTP maps, we proved that the same is not always true, with many counterexamples. It is known that the category of CPTP maps is semicartesian. We add to this a classification of binary products. We proved that products only exist in trivial cases. For binary coproducts, we obtained the dimensions they should have, if they exist. In both cases, topological techniques were used. For this reason, we also investigated the topology of the closure of the set of extreme points of the CPTP maps. Topological invariants can be computed from decompositions of a space such as CW structures. These are a type of decomposition in which the space is expressed as a gluing of balls. In the search for a CW structure for the closure of the set of extreme CPTP maps, we obtained a partial decomposition that resembles a CW structure of the complex projective space.

</details>


### [112] [Axiomatization of the Levin--Wen Wave Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21109)
*Zhengwei Liu,Zishuo Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Levin-Wen模型的长期开放问题，提出了六个公理来内在地刻画Levin-Wen基态波函数，无需预先假设范畴对称数据或哈密顿量，并能重构出对应的酉融合范畴，证明所得波函数映射到涌现范畴的Levin-Wen基态向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levin-Wen模型提供了基于酉融合范畴的拓扑序格点实现，但长期以来的开放问题是：如何在不预设范畴对称数据或哈密顿量的情况下，内在地刻画Levin-Wen基态波函数。

Method: 提出六个公理，作用于定义在多尺度格点上的波函数族；通过这些公理重构出潜在的酉融合范畴，并建立从波函数到Levin-Wen基态向量的映射。

Result: 证明了满足六个公理的波函数族可以唯一重构出酉融合范畴，且这些波函数映射到该涌现范畴的非零Levin-Wen基态向量。

Conclusion: 该工作为Levin-Wen基态波函数提供了不依赖哈密顿量或先验对称数据的内在刻画，推动了拓扑序的纯波函数层面理解。

Abstract: The Levin--Wen model provides a lattice realization of topological orders associated with a given unitary fusion category. A longstanding open problem is to characterize Levin--Wen ground-state wave functions intrinsically, without assuming a priori categorical symmetry data or a Hamiltonian. We address this by proposing six axioms on a family of wave functions defined on lattices at multiple scales. These axioms allow us to reconstruct the underlying unitary fusion category and prove that the resulting wave functions map to nonzero Levin--Wen ground-state vectors of the emergent category.

</details>


### [113] [Bethe-root configurations and spectral degeneracy in the open XXZ chain with degenerate boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20815)
*Shizhang Wu,Shu Chen,Junpeng Cao,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了自旋1/2 XXZ链在简并开放边界条件下Bethe根构型的结构，对完全简并和部分简并情形下的物理解进行了分类，并提出了各类物理解集合的计数公式，同时揭示了完全简并情形下由幻影弦引起的谱简并现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放边界条件下可积模型的研究对理解量子可积系统的物理性质具有重要意义，而Bethe根构型的结构直接影响谱的性质。此前关于XXZ链简并边界情形的Bethe根分类和谱简并机制尚不清晰，需要系统的理论分析。

Method: 通过对Bethe Ansatz方程物理解的深入分析，将完全简并情形下的解分为三类，部分简并情形下的解分为两类；针对每类解提出计数公式；并通过分析幻影弦在Bethe根中的存在性来解释完全简并情形下观测到的谱简并现象。此外还讨论了对角极限下的分类和谱简并。

Result: 在完全简并情形下，Bethe根物理解被划分为三种类型，部分简并情形下划分为两种类型，并给出了各类物理解集合的准确计数公式。完全简并情形下观测到谱简并，且这些简并可归因于Bethe根中特定幻影弦的存在。对角极限下的分类与谱简并也得到了一致的结果。

Conclusion: 本文为简并开放边界XXZ链的Bethe根结构提供了完整的分类和计数框架，并通过幻影弦机制成功解释了谱简并现象，加深了对开放边界可积模型中边界简并与体态谱之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of Bethe-root configurations in the spin-1/2 XXZ chain with degenerate open boundaries. The physical solutions of the Bethe Ansatz equations are classified into three types in the fully degenerate case and two types in the partially degenerate case, for which we propose counting formulas for the number of physical solution sets of each type. Furthermore, we observe spectral degeneracies in the fully degenerate case and show that they can be naturally explained by the presence of specific phantom strings in the Bethe roots. The classification and resulting spectral degeneracies in the diagonal limit are also discussed.

</details>


### [114] [The transverse matter Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21211)
*Andrea Marini,Kai Wu,Riccardo Reho*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了费米1932年用经典高斯方程推导库仑相互作用的经典方法，将其推广到矢量势的横向分量，通过全量子正则变换将横向矢量势替换为电流-电流和电流-电流-密度相互作用，得到纯物质、完全不相干且规范不变的哈密顿量，并展示了其在声子等元激发纵横分裂中的量子起源作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米程序仅处理了纵向电磁势（库仑相互作用），而横向矢量势的量子处理尚未被纳入该框架。此外，相干性可能导致Haag定理所预示的微扰论失效，需要一种能避免该崩溃并允许引入图方法的变换方案。

Method: 采用全量子正则变换，将横向矢量势替换为电流-电流和电流-电流-密度相互作用，然后将变换后的哈密顿量投影到费米子空间，获得纯物质的、不相干的、规范不变的哈密顿量。

Result: 成功获得了一个物质-only、完全不相干且规范不变的哈密顿量，避免了Haag定理导致的微扰论崩溃，可在变换空间中引入图方法。同时证明了横向哈密顿量提供了电磁纵横劈裂（如声子和元激发）的量子起源。

Conclusion: 该工作为电磁相互作用的费米程序提供了完整的量子扩展，将横向矢量势纳入正则变换框架，为理解元激发的纵横劈裂提供了量子层面的理论基础。

Abstract: Enrico Fermi in 1932 used classical Gauss equation to derive the Coulomb density--density interaction from the longitudinal electromagnetic potential. In this work we extend the Fermi procedure to the transverse component of the vector potential. By using a fully quantum canonical transformation, we replace the transverse vector potential with a current--current and current--current--density interactions. The transformed Hamiltonian is, then, projected in the fermionic space providing a matter--only, totally incoherent and gauge respecting Hamiltonian. The removal of coherence will avoid the breakdown of perturbation theory predicted by Haag's theorem, and make possible to introduce, in the transformed space, the diagrammatic approach. After discussing the implications of the transverse interactions on the current theories based on the Fermi procedure, we conclude by showing how the transverse Hamiltonian provides the quantum origin of the electromagnetic longitudinal--transverse splitting of phonons and other elemental excitations.

</details>


### [115] [Spectral gaps for mean-field Coulomb gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21215)
*Simon Becker,Angeliki Menegaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维Coulomb气体（d≥3）在粒子数上均匀的Poincaré不等式，其中逆温度β_N可依赖于N，并在以有效相互作用强度Θ_N表示的弱耦合条件下成立，由此导出过阻尼Langevin动力学的指数松弛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coulomb气体在数学物理和统计物理中具有重要地位，其平衡性质的定量估计（如Poincaré不等式）对理解其动力学行为和收敛速率至关重要。已有结果通常要求β固定或满足特定约束，作者希望放宽条件，允许β_N随N变化，并给出均匀于粒子数的谱隙下界。

Method: 核心方法是三部分：首先建立单粒子位置的Poincaré不等式，其对Coulomb极点数量及位置（含重合极点情形）均匀成立；其次通过移动极点估计（moving-pole estimate）与Dobrushin-Wu张量化技巧将单点估计提升为多粒子下界；最后利用质心坐标得到匹配的上界。

Result: 在弱耦合条件（以有效相互作用强度Θ_N刻画）下，得到了关于粒子数N均匀的Poincaré不等式（下界与上界匹配），从而建立了相应过阻尼Langevin动力学的指数松弛速率。关键创新在于单点估计对含重合极点的Coulomb势均匀成立。

Conclusion: 该结果克服了先前工作中对β固定或强耦合的限制，将Coulomb气体谱隙估计推广到β_N依赖N的一般情形，为研究此类系统的非平衡动力学与热化速率提供了定量工具。

Abstract: We prove a Poincaré inequality, uniform in the particle number, for repulsive (one-component) Coulomb gases in dimensions $d\ge3$, with inverse temperature $β_N$ that is allowed to depend on $N$. We assume a weak-coupling condition expressed in terms of the effective interaction strength $Θ_N$ seen by a single particle. This yields exponential relaxation for the associated overdamped Langevin dynamics. The main ingredient is a one-site Poincaré inequality uniform in the number and positions of the Coulomb poles, including coincident poles. A moving-pole estimate and Dobrushin-Wu tensorization yield the many-particle lower bound, while the center-of-mass coordinate gives the matching upper bound.

</details>


### [116] [The spin-1/2 Heisenberg XXZ chain and the Lorentz mirror model with loop weight 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21306)
*Kieran Ryan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了自旋1/2 Heisenberg XXZ链在Δ∈[-1,1/2]范围内，环面基态收敛到无穷体积基态，自旋-自旋关联函数呈多项式衰减。在Δ∈[-1,0]范围内还有更强结果，包括有限体积有限温度态的收敛、极限可交换、动态关联函数收敛等。方法不依赖Bethe Ansatz，而是通过Lorentz镜模型与六顶角模型等概率模型的耦合证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋1/2 Heisenberg XXZ链是凝聚态物理和数学物理中最重要的可积模型之一。虽然基态在无穷体积下的收敛已通过Bethe Ansatz方法严格证明，但关联函数的多项式衰减（除Δ=0,-1外）、动态关联函数的收敛以及极限交换性在严格层面仍是新的。作者希望发展不依赖Bethe Ansatz的新方法，通过概率模型（Lorentz镜模型、六顶角模型）来证明这些结果。

Method: 作者不使用Bethe Ansatz技术，而是采用以下概率模型方法：(1) 利用Lorentz镜模型（环权为2的随机环模型）在大范围参数下证明连接概率趋向0并在对称情形下多项式衰减；(2) 通过两个Lorentz镜模型与六顶角模型的耦合（其中一个是新的），结合六顶角模型高度函数的去局域化结果；(3) 进一步证明六顶角模型时空版本的高度函数去局域化，并通过类似耦合证明XXZ模型环表示中连接概率收敛到0。

Result: (1) 在Δ∈[-1,1/2]范围内，XXZ链环面基态收敛到无穷体积基态，自旋-自旋关联⟨S₀⁽¹⁾Sₓ⁽¹⁾⟩关于x多项式衰减。(2) 在Δ∈[-1,0]范围内，有限体积有限温度态收敛，L与β=1/T可以任意顺序趋于无穷，动态(Euclidean)关联函数也收敛，且⟨S₀⁽¹⁾Sₓ⁽¹⁾(t)⟩在|(x,t)|趋于无穷时衰减到0且不可能指数衰减。(3) 除了Δ=0,-1的点，关联衰减结果、动态关联收敛及极限可交换性均为严格数学上全新的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作通过概率耦合方法（而非Bethe Ansatz）为XXZ链建立了若干新的严格结果，包括关联函数多项式衰减和动态关联的收敛。这些结果表明概率模型（Lorentz镜模型与六顶角模型）可作为研究量子自旋链严格性质的有力新工具。

Abstract: We prove that for the spin-1/2 Heisenberg XXZ chain in the range $Δ\in[-1,1/2]$, the ground state on the torus of length $L$ converges to an infinite volume ground state $\langle\cdot\rangle$ as $L\to\infty$, and that the spin-spin correlation $\langle S_0^{(1)}S_x^{(1)}\rangle$ decays polynomially fast in $x$. In the range $Δ\in[-1,0]$ we have the stronger results: that the convergence holds for several finite-volume, finite temperature states, that $L$ and $β=1/T$ can be taken to infinity in any order, that the convergence holds on (Euclidean) dynamic correlators, and that $\langle S_0^{(1)}S_x^{(1)}(t)\rangle$ decays to zero in $|(x,t)|$, and cannot decay exponentially fast.
  The convergence of the ground state in infinite volume is known rigorously by Bethe Ansatz methods, while our correlation decay results (apart from the points $Δ=0,-1$), the convergence on dynamic correlators and the interchangeability of limits are new at the rigorous level. Moreover our methods are new and do not use any Bethe Ansatz techniques, using only the following related probabilistic models.
  In the Lorentz mirror model with loop weight 2, a model of random loops on the square lattice, in a large range of parameters we prove that connection probabilities tend to 0, and do so polynomially fast in the symmetric case of the model. Our proof uses two couplings of this mirror model with the six-vertex model, one of which is new. The main input is the delocalisation of the height function of the six-vertex model proved by several authors.
  We further prove that the height function of a certain space-time version of the six-vertex model delocalises and then prove through analogous couplings to those mentioned above that connection probabilities converge to 0 in the loop representation of the XXZ model introduced by Ueltschi.

</details>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Slope stability of tangent bundles of smooth toric Fano varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20411)
*Bernd Johannes Wuebben*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对所有8630个三至六维光滑环流形Fano簇的切丛反典线斜率稳定性进行了完整分类，通过精确计算Klyachko判据确定每个严格半稳定情形下的极稳定性（等价于存在反典Kähler形式的Hermitian-Einstein度量），并发现了一个新的高Picard秩构造——根扭转环流形dP纤维化，产生稳定的切丛。稳定的环流形Fano簇在Picard秩为n+2时对每个n≥4都存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Fano簇中切丛稳定性与Kähler-Einstein度量存在性之间的关系是代数几何和微分几何的核心问题。虽然已知Kähler-Einstein簇的切丛是极稳定的，但反过来是否成立一直不明确。作者希望通过穷举所有低维光滑环流形Fano簇，系统性地检验这两个性质之间的精确联系，并发现新的结构。

Method: 作者使用Klyachko判据对8630个光滑环流形Fano簇的切丛反典线斜率稳定性进行精确计算和分类。对于新引入的根扭转环流形dP纤维化族，将斜率不等式化简为A2矩六边形上的积分，利用正层分解和尖锐的一维卷积估计证明稳定性，通过六边形第一矩在极值指数处的严格序关系得出Kähler-Einstein分类。

Result: 1) 对所有8630个三至六维光滑环流形Fano簇完成了切丛斜率稳定性的完整分类；2) 每个Kähler-Einstein簇的切丛都是极稳定的，但逆命题广泛不成立——109个五维稳定切丛簇中有102个不是Kähler-Einstein；3) 发现了根扭转环流形dP纤维化一族，零和根扭转产生稳定切丛，仅在多集关于负对称或三重旋转对称时才Kähler-Einstein；4) 证明了Picard秩为n+2的稳定环流形Fano簇对每个n≥4都存在。

Conclusion: 切丛斜率稳定性与Kähler-Einstein性质之间存在显著差距：稳定性是Kähler-Einstein的必要但远非充分条件。新的根扭转构造揭示了零扭转和条件既不充分也非必要，为理解高Picard秩Fano簇的几何结构提供了新的视角和反例族。

Abstract: We classify the anticanonical slope stability of tangent bundles for all $8{,}630$ smooth toric Fano varieties in dimensions three through six, by exact evaluation of Klyachko's criterion, and determine polystability (equivalently, the existence of a Hermitian--Einstein metric with respect to an anticanonical Kähler form) in every strictly semistable case. EveryKähler--Einstein variety in the census has polystable tangent bundle, whereas the converse fails widely: $102$ of the $109$ five-folds with stable tangent bundle are not Kähler--Einstein.
  The census singles out one construction at high Picard rank, which we introduce in general: root-twisted toric $\mathrm{dP}$-fibrations over products of projective lines, parametrized by roots of $A_2$. Every nonempty multiset of nonzero root twists with vanishing sum produces a stable tangent bundle, in every dimension. Among these zero-sum twists, the resulting variety is Kähler--Einstein if and only if the multiset is invariant under negation or under the order-three rotation of the root hexagon. Thus stable toric Fanos of Picard rank $n+2$ exist for every $n\ge4$; vanishing twist sum does not force the Kähler--Einstein property, while a separate unbalanced family shows that a vanishing sum is not necessary for stability.
  The proof reduces the slope inequalities for the root-twist family to integrals over the $A_2$ moment hexagon. Positive layer decompositions and sharp one-dimensional convolution estimates establish stability, while a strict ordering of the hexagon's first moments at the extreme exponents yields the Kähler--Einstein classification.

</details>


### [118] [Submanifolds of Bochner Holomorphic Statistical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20499)
*Harmandeep Kaur,Gauree Shanker*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有消失Bochner曲率张量的全纯统计流形（Bochner全纯统计流形）的子流形，包括拉格朗日统计子流形的共形平坦性，以及全纯子流形中双重自平行子流形的Bochner曲率张量消失性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统计流形理论中，Bochner曲率张量的消失条件对流形的几何结构有重要意义。作者旨在通过研究Bochner全纯统计流形的各类子流形，揭示其在特定条件下（如拉格朗日子流形、双重自平行全纯子流形）的几何性质，特别是共形平坦性与曲率张量的行为。

Method: 论文采用微分几何的标准方法，分别研究两类子流形：(1) Bochner全纯统计流形中的拉格朗日统计子流形，在其Bochner曲率张量消失的假设下分析共形平坦性；(2) Bochner全纯统计流形的全纯子流形，通过引入双重自平行的概念，证明该子流形保持Bochner曲率张量消失的性质。

Result: 研究证明了两个主要结果：其一，在Bochner曲率张量消失的假设下，拉格朗日统计子流形具有共形平坦性；其二，Bochner全纯统计流形的双重自平行全纯子流形的Bochner曲率张量也消失，即该性质在双重自平行全纯子流形中保持。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Bochner全纯统计流形结构中曲率性质的遗传性：Bochner曲率张量的消失性质可以传递给某些特定类型的子流形（如双重自平行全纯子流形），同时拉格朗日统计子流形在此条件下表现出共形平坦的几何特征，丰富了全纯统计流形的子流形几何理论。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the submanifolds of holomorphic statistical manifolds with vanishing Bochner curvature tensor (Bochner holomorphic statistical manifolds). We study the Lagrangian statistical submanifolds and discuss their conformal flatness under the assumption of vanishing Bochner curvature tensor. Next we study the holomorphic submanifolds of Bochner holomorphic statistical manifolds and prove that the doubly autoparallel holomorphic submanifold of a Bochner holomorphic statistical manifold has vanishing Bochner curvature tensor.

</details>


### [119] [Ricci Soliton Classification on $\mathbb H^2\times\mathbb R$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20503)
*Anton Khaliapin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准度量下黎曼流形 H²×R 上的 Ricci 孤立子，完整分类了孤立子向量场：它们构成一个四维仿射空间，即 Killing 代数 isom(H²×R) 的平移；所有对应孤立子均为膨胀型；梯度孤立子被完全刻画，构成完整孤立子族的一参数子族；此外，所有孤立子向量场都是仿射的，保持 Levi-Civita 联络、曲率张量和 Ricci 张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ricci 孤立子是 Ricci 流的自相似解，在几何分析中具有重要意义。作者希望针对具有积结构流形 H²×R 这一具体且重要的几何背景，系统分类其上的孤立子向量场，以深化对该流形 Ricci 流行为的理解。

Method: 利用 H²×R 的标准调和度量结构，结合 Killing 代数与孤立子方程，通过解析方法对孤立子向量场进行完整分类，并进一步刻画梯度孤立子子族的结构。

Result: 孤立子向量场构成四维仿射空间（Killing 代数的平移）；所有孤立子均为膨胀型；梯度孤立子构成一参数子族；且所有孤立子向量场均保持联络、曲率张量与 Ricci 张量（即仿射性）。

Conclusion: 在 H²×R 上，Ricci 孤立子的分类完全由 Killing 代数决定，且所有孤立子均为膨胀型，梯度情形仅是其中一参数子族。该结果揭示了该流形上孤立子结构的代数本质与几何刚性。

Abstract: We study Ricci solitons on the Riemannian manifold $\mathbb H^2\times\mathbb R$ equipped with the standard metric. A complete classification of soliton vector fields is obtained: they form a four-dimensional affine space, namely a translate of the Killing algebra $\mathfrak{isom}(\mathbb H^2\times\mathbb R)$. All corresponding solitons are expanding. In addition, gradient solitons are fully characterized and shown to form a one-parameter subfamily of the complete family of soliton fields. As a byproduct, every soliton vector field turns out to be affine, preserving the Levi-Civita connection, the curvature tensor, and the Ricci tensor.

</details>


### [120] [Almost Positive Sectional Curvature with Bounded Geometry on $S^2\times S^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20888)
*Yuhang Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在$S^2\times S^2$上存在一个固定的光滑开球$U$，使得对任意$\varepsilon>0$，都能构造一个光滑度量$g_\varepsilon$，其在除去一个相对体积任意小的闭集$S_\varepsilon$外具有正截面曲率，且在$U$上所有截面曲率都大于$1$，同时保持体积、直径和单射半径的一致有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在四维流形$S^2\times S^2$上能否构造"几乎处处正曲率"且在某固定区域上曲率>1的度量，同时保持一致的有界几何量（体积、直径、单射半径下界），从而探讨曲率受限下几何结构的灵活性。

Method: 从单位圆乘积度量出发，通过三步构造：(1) 用一个光滑化的高度函数绝对值作为小共形因子，使混合截面平坦方向变为严格正曲率（除两薄赤道带外），赤道带内产生的负曲率仅为$O(\varepsilon)$；(2) 在一个很小区域内进行第二次共形变形，产生一个所有截面曲率均大于$1$的核心区域，其过渡区Hessian上界为$O(\varepsilon)$；(3) 用一个微分同胚将固定拓扑开球$U$压缩进该核心区域，通过拉回保持所有内蕴有界几何估计。

Result: 成功构造了满足所有要求的度量$g_\varepsilon$：在$M\setminus S_\varepsilon$上正截面曲率，$S_\varepsilon$相对体积小于$\varepsilon$，$U$上所有截面曲率>1，负曲率下界为$-\varepsilon$，正曲率上界为常数$\Lambda$（与$\varepsilon$无关），体积和直径有一致正上下界，单射半径有一致正下界，且$U\cap S_\varepsilon=\varnothing$。

Conclusion: 该构造表明在$S^2\times S^2$上可以在任意给定区域实现"曲率>1"且全局几乎处处正曲率的度量，同时保持几何量的均匀有界性，展示了奇点集可被压缩至任意小体积而核心正能量区域固定不变的几何构造能力。注：本论文主要内容由ChatGPT 5.6生成并经作者验证。

Abstract: Let $M=S^2\times S^2$. We prove that there is a fixed smooth open ball $U\subset M$ such that, for every $\varepsilon>0$, one can find a smooth metric $g_\varepsilon$ and a closed set $S_\varepsilon$ with the following properties: $\sec_{g_\varepsilon}>0$ on $M\setminus S_\varepsilon$; the relative volume of $S_\varepsilon$ is less than $\varepsilon$; every sectional curvature on $U$ is greater than $1$; $-\varepsilon<\sec_{g_\varepsilon}\leqΛ$ for one constant $Λ$ independent of $\varepsilon$; the volume and diameter have uniform positive lower and upper bounds; and the injectivity radius has a uniform positive lower bound. In fact, $U\cap S_\varepsilon=\varnothing$.
  The construction starts from the product of the unit round metrics. A small conformal factor, obtained by smoothing the absolute value of the height function on each sphere, has negative definite Hessian away from two thin equatorial bands. It makes every formerly flat mixed plane strictly positive outside a set of arbitrarily small relative volume, while the negative curvature created in the bands is only $O(\varepsilon)$. A second conformal deformation supported in a very small region produces a core on which all sectional curvatures are greater than $1$; its transition has Hessian bounded above by $O(\varepsilon)$. Finally, a diffeomorphism compresses the fixed topological ball $U$ into that core. Pullback preserves all intrinsic bounded-geometry estimates. The main content of this paper is generated by ChatGPT 5.6 and verified by the author.

</details>


### [121] [Radial pinching and topological rigidity for free boundary Gaussian $f$-minimal submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20923)
*Niang Chen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧氏闭球内自由边界f_c-极小浸入子流形的拓扑分类问题。在曲率条件cR²≤k和关于第二基本形式的法向分量的pinching条件下，证明了M微分同胚于D^k或S¹×D^{k-1}，严格pinching下为D^k。在二维余维一情形，非圆盘分支具有旋转对称性，并构造了局部嵌入旋转例子族（含临界悬链面作为c=0成员）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由边界极小曲面/子流形是几何分析中的重要研究对象。本文试图在球内f_c-极小（即加权面积泛函的临界点）的框架下，推广经典的Simon猜想（紧致嵌入自由边界极小曲面在球内若满足一定曲率条件则必为圆盘或环带的位移）。作者希望通过对第二基本形式垂直于x方向的法向分量进行pinching，得到更强的拓扑限制，并刻画非圆盘分支的对称性质。

Method: 证明方法基于三个关键步骤：(1) 利用平方距离函数的Hessian凸性来分析M在球内的位置与形态；(2) 在平方距离函数的极小集合上进行零化空间（nullity）估计，借此控制M的截面曲率与法向量的约束；(3) 通过子水平集（sublevel-set）论证来推断M的拓扑结构。此外，利用旋转变量的对称性分析处理二维余维一的情形，并通过显式构造证明了嵌入旋转例子族的存在性。

Result: 在pinching条件|A_{x^\perp}|²≤1+(k-1)^{-1}(1-c|x^\perp|²)²下，证明M要么微分同胚于k维圆盘，要么微分同胚于S¹×D^{k-1}；在严格pinching下退化为圆盘。二维余维一情形中非圆盘分支具有旋转对称性；构造了局部嵌入旋转例子族，其c=0极限为临界悬链面。

Conclusion: 本文在f_c-极小自由边界框架下给出了Simon猜想的加权推广，得到与经典结果一致的拓扑分类（圆盘或环带），并在严格pinching下唯一化为圆盘。旋转对称性结果的建立和临界悬链面作为极限例子的构造，提供了对pinching常数最优性的深刻理解，为后续在更一般加权极小曲面上的对称性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $M^k\subset \overline{B}_R^N$ be a smooth compact connected orientable free boundary $f_c$-minimal submanifold of the closed Euclidean ball, where $f_c(x)=c|x|^2/2$ and $c\ge 0$. Assume that $cR^2\le k$ and $|A_{x^\perp}|^2\le 1+\frac{1}{k-1}(1-c|x^\perp|^2)^2$, where $A_{x^\perp}(X,Y)=\langle x^\perp,A(X,Y)\rangle$. We prove that $M$ is diffeomorphic either to $D^k$ or to $S^1\times D^{k-1}$; strict pinching yields the disk. The proof uses Hessian convexity of the squared-distance function, a nullity estimate along its minimum set, and a sublevel-set argument. In dimension two and codimension one, the non-disk branch is rotationally symmetric. We also construct a local family of embedded rotational examples for small $c\ge 0$, with the $c=0$ member equal to the critical catenoid.

</details>


### [122] [Volume gap for minimal submanifolds in spheres, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21144)
*Jianquan Ge,Fagui Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了球面中闭连通非全测地极小浸入的体积下界估计。作者证明了当浸入点具有多重性m时，流形体积至少为 [m + ε_n Σ(S(x_j)/S_*)^2]·Vol(S^n)，其中ε_n介于特定常数之间。对于线性满浸入，体积比有更精细的下界。此外还给出了关于超平面划分的每个连通分量的体积下界，从而限制了分量的最大数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小浸入的几何研究中，体积的定量下界是理解浸入整体结构的重要工具。特别是当浸入点具有多重性时，如何利用第二基本形式的模长信息（S）来获得更精确的体积估计，以及如何利用这些估计来控制浸入的拓扑和几何结构，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 该方法结合了第二基本形式模长S的极值分析、多重性参数m的积分几何技巧，以及线性满浸入的维度约束。通过对浸入点处S值的加权求和，构造出包含ε_n系数的最优体积下界；再利用超平面截面对连通分量的体积进行估计。

Result: 得到了三组主要结果：(1) 带多重性m和非全测地压制的体积下界 Vol(M) ≥ [m + ε_n Σ(S(x_j)/S_*)^2]Vol(S^n)，其中常数ε_n的范围为 [110n(n+2)^2]^{-1} 到 [104n(n+2)^2]^{-1}；(2) 线性满浸入的体积比下界为 max{1+ε_n, 4(n+1)^n(n+3)^{-(n+2)}(n+q+1)}；(3) 任意过原点超平面H的补集M\f^{-1}(H)每个连通分量体积至少为 4(n+1)^n(n+3)^{-(n+2)}Vol(S^n)，从而给出分量数目的上界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了球面中极小浸入的体积与其多重性、第二基本形式模长之间的定量关系，给出了显式的数值常数下界，并通过超平面截断获得了连通分量数目的控制。这些结果深化了对球面极小浸入几何结构的理解，具有重要的几何分析意义。

Abstract: Let $f:M^n\looparrowright\Sph^{n+q}(1)$, $n\ge2$ and $q\ge1$, be a closed, connected, non-totally-geodesic minimal immersion with second fundamental form $h$, and put $S=|h|^2$ and $S_*=\max_M S$. If $p\in f(M)$ has multiplicity $m$ and $f^{-1}(p)=\{x_1,\ldots,x_m\}$, then \[
  \Vol(M)\ge
  \left[m+\varepsilon_n\sum_{j=1}^m
  \left(\frac{S(x_j)}{S_*}\right)^2\right]\Vol(\Sph^n), \] where $[110n(n+2)^2]^{-1}<\varepsilon_n<[104n(n+2)^2]^{-1}$. If the immersion is linearly full, then \[
  \frac{\Vol(M)}{\Vol(\Sph^n)}
  \ge
  \max\!\left\{1+\varepsilon_n,
  \frac{4(n+1)^n}{(n+3)^{n+2}}(n+q+1)\right\}. \] Moreover, for every hyperplane $H$ through the origin, each connected component of $M\setminus f^{-1}(H)$ has volume at least $4(n+1)^n(n+3)^{-n-2}\Vol(\Sph^n)$; consequently the number of components is at most $\frac{(n+3)^{n+2}}{4(n+1)^n}\frac{\Vol(M)}{\Vol(\Sph^n)}$.

</details>


### [123] [Differential Harnack Estimates for the Filtration Equations on Riemannian Manifolds via Nash--Moser Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21213)
*Jian-Hua Hao,Yu-Zhao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了完备黎曼流形上过滤方程 u_t=ΔF(u) 在一致抛物范围内的光滑正解的局部微分 Harnack 估计，通过 Bochner-判别式方法得到正部强制不等式，并以 Nash-Moser 迭代封闭该不等式，由此推导出 Harnack 与 Liouville 推论，并恢复了多孔介质方程和快扩散方程的 Aronson-Bénilan 估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分 Harnack 估计是研究非线性抛物方程解的正则性和长时间行为的关键工具。对于一般的过滤方程（非幂律非线性函数 F），现有的微分 Harnack 估计结果较为有限，作者希望建立适用于一般非线性过滤规律的统一框架。

Method: 采用 Bochner-判别式方法推导出关于解的梯度量的 coercive 正部不等式（即局部微分 Harnack 不等式），该不等式的封闭性通过 Nash-Moser 迭代技术实现。作者在一致抛物（uniformly parabolic）范围内处理一般非线性的过滤方程。

Result: 获得了过滤方程光滑正解的局部微分 Harnack 估计，并由此推导出 Harnack 不等式和 Liouville 定理等推论；恢复并推广了多孔介质方程与快扩散方程的已知 Aronson-Bénilan 估计，同时刻画了一类非幂律型的过滤规律（genuinely non-power filtration laws）也适用该估计框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将微分 Harnack 估计从经典的幂律非线性过滤方程推广到更一般的非线性过滤方程，在一致抛物范围内建立了统一的估计框架，为更广泛的退化/奇异抛物方程研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We prove local differential Harnack estimates for smooth positive solutions of
  the Filtration Equations $u_t=ΔF(u)$ on complete Riemannian manifolds in uniformly parabolic
  ranges. A Bochner--discriminant argument gives a coercive positive-part
  inequality, which is closed by Nash--Moser iteration. We derive Harnack and
  Liouville consequences, recover the Aronson-Bénilan estimates for porous medium equation and fast diffusion equation, and
  describe a class of genuinely non-power filtration laws.

</details>


### [124] [Stable $s$-minimal cones in $\mathbb{R}^3$ are flat for $s$ close to zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21340)
*Raphael Tsiamis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明在三维空间中，当s充分趋近于零时，稳定的非局部极小锥是平坦的。作者发展了当s→0时稳定s-极小曲面在所有维度上的新结构性质，包括紧致性理论和靠近半空间的夹紧定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非局部极小曲面的几何性质，特别是探索当s趋近于零时稳定锥的平坦性问题，这是非局部几何测度论中的核心问题。

Method: 开发了s→0时稳定s-极小曲面的新结构性质，包括紧致性理论（compactness theory）和靠近半空间的夹紧定理（pinching theorems），通过这些工具证明三维空间中稳定非局部极小锥的平坦性。

Result: 证明了在三维空间中，对于充分接近零的s值，稳定的非局部极小锥是平坦的（flat）。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了非局部极小曲面理论中关于稳定锥平坦性的空白，为理解s→0极限行为提供了新的结构框架，且方法适用于所有维度而不仅限于三维。

Abstract: We prove that stable nonlocal minimal cones in three dimensions are flat, for $s$ sufficiently close to zero. Our approach develops new structural properties of stable $s$-minimal surfaces in every dimension as $s \downarrow 0$, notably a compactness theory and pinching theorems near a half-space.

</details>
